\section*{eid\_\allowbreak{}hawkes\_\allowbreak{}fixedbin\_\allowbreak{}dealias}

\noindent\textit{Specialization:} v1\par

\paragraph{TL;DR.} At every fixed known bin width \(\Delta>0\), coarse timestamping does not erase the labeled Hawkes mechanism in the stated feed-forward model: the mean and two positive covariance lags recover \(\operatorname{im}(A)\), the common decay, every amplitude, the baselines, and hence \(G(A)\), including singular matrices, repeated poles, disconnected components, diamonds, and proportional rows. A broader exact population theorem covers every nonzero stable common-exponential mechanism whose generator restricted to \(\operatorname{im}(A)\) lies in the principal-log strip, including feedback, self-excitation, and reciprocal edges. Its inverse first reconstructs the complete covariance response from the two lags and then uses a finite matrix-resolvent calculation plus one uniquely characterized positive scalar root; Wiener--Hopf uniqueness certifies that root but is not an unevaluated computational step. The covariance-only map first recovers \(D\) and the mean from \(\Gamma_0\). The empty graph is exactly the \(\beta\)-nonidentified stratum. Rank-adaptive estimation, vanishing-threshold support recovery, and conservative simultaneous graph confidence sets remain proved only for the acyclic margin class. One-lag injectivity on the named three-node motifs and honest adaptation over the nuisance-regular closure remain open.

\section{Project justification}

\paragraph{Gap.} Published Hawkes identification results use the complete continuous conditional response, full spectral or Laplace-transform information, latent-time likelihood, higher cumulants, the full aggregated-count distribution, or a vanishing-mesh approximation. BacryMuzy2016 proves uniqueness of the kernel given the complete response but does not reconstruct that response from two fixed-bin covariance lags or reduce the resulting inverse to a finite scalar root. Koyama2025 gives an \(O(\Delta t^3)\) small-bin spectral approximation, and Kirchner2016 proves convergence of an INAR\((\infty)\) approximation as \(\Delta\to0\); neither gives an exact inverse at fixed positive bin width.

\paragraph{Niche.} The contribution-defining acyclic model turns fixed-bin de-aliasing into explicit nilpotent algebra and supports rank-adaptive inference. At population level, the same two-lag covariance semigroup also exposes the possibly singular active image for stable feedback mechanisms. Under only a principal-log strip on that image, the complete response can be reconstructed before a finite response transform separates the common exponential decay from the excitation matrix.

\paragraph{Fill.} The project derives the exact bin-covariance semigroup and image identity, gives the explicit acyclic inverse through a restricted finite logarithm and antisymmetric jump, and characterizes \(A=0\) as the exact nonidentified decay stratum. Its broad population extension reconstructs \(C_+(t)\), forms \(\mathcal R^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)\) by an \(r\)-dimensional resolvent, and proves that two finite matrix equations have the unique solution \((x,M)=(\beta,A)\) by using Wiener--Hopf uniqueness only as a certificate. The zero-lag identity yields a covariance-only version. Rank-adaptive estimation, support recovery, and simultaneous graph uncertainty continue to use the explicit acyclic branches and their centered composite Jacobian; no broad feedback-class sample inference is claimed.

\section{Related work}

BacryMuzy2016 is the canonical identification ancestor. Section II-B, Proposition 2 and Equation (18), gives the complete-lag Wiener--Hopf system; Theorem 1 and Equation (19) give its unique causal \(L^1\) solution, and Corollary 1 identifies the process from complete first- and second-order statistics. Here that uniqueness theorem is used only to certify a candidate produced by two finite matrix equations: the complete response itself is first reconstructed exactly from one mean and two fixed-bin covariance lags, and the evaluator reduces to a matrix resolvent and a unique positive scalar root. Koyama2025 Section 3.4, page 8, Theorem 2 and Equation (18), and Corollary 1 give third-order small-bin spectral approximations, while Section 3.5, Equations (19)--(20), defines a quadratic-form estimator for the coarse-grained representation; these results do not invert the original process exactly at fixed positive \(\Delta\). Kirchner2016 Section 3.2, Theorem 2 and Equation (21), constructs an INAR\((\infty)\)-based approximation converging as \(\Delta\to0\); its lag equations likewise do not furnish a fixed-positive-bin inverse. EtesamiZhangSinghalKiyavash2016 uses full spectral zeros and covariance-density Laplace transforms. ZhouPapadogeorgou2025 uses the full aggregated-count law from at least three bins. ShlomovichCohenAdams2022 uses latent-time Monte Carlo expectation maximization, and QiaoCaiWuXiangZhangHao2023 uses a separate discrete structural model for low-resolution cellular alarms. CheyssonLang2022 gives the aliased full bin-count spectrum and Whittle inference under full-spectral injectivity, not this two-lag population inverse. Hawkes1971Spectra and AchabBacryGaiffasMastromatteoMuzy2017 inform the covariance-only identities. WangXieCuozzoMakXie2020 and WangKolarShojaie2025 use exact event histories for inference, so neither supplies graph-wide fixed-bin sign inversion. BolyCheyssonNguyen2024 supplies Hawkes mixing background; the acyclic finite-depth argument here supplies the paper's inference-side mixing bound. GleserHwang1987 motivates the still-open bounded-metric adaptation frontier, and KoltchinskiiLounici2016Projectors and ZhangWu2017 inform the acyclic projector and dependent-data Gaussian arguments. JinHuang2026 uses covariance rank in a vanishing-bin autoregressive limit and does not subsume exact recovery at fixed \(\Delta\).

\section{Setup}

Target (estimand / structural parameter): \((\beta,A,\mu)\), with the labeled causal graph \(G(A)\) recovered exactly.
Primary functional / estimator / diagnostic: On \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\), \((\beta,A,\mu)=\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\), where \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\) reconstructs the complete response from two positive lags, evaluates a finite matrix resolvent, and selects the unique positive scalar root; on \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\), \((\beta,A,\mu)=\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH},0}(\Gamma_0,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\), where \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH},0}\) first reconstructs \(D\) and \(m\) and then invokes the same finite evaluator. These broad images contain every nonzero law in \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{stab}}\) whose restricted generator \(\Delta(A-\beta I_p)|_{\operatorname{im}(A)}\) lies in the principal-log strip. The four explicit algebraic branches remain: on \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG},+}\), \((\beta,A,\mu)=\Phi_{\Delta,r}(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)=\Psi_{\Delta,r}(\Gamma_0,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\) with \(r=\operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1)\); on \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\) and \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\), the corresponding maps are \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\) and \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs},0}\); on \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\) and \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\), they are \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\) and \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr},0}\); and on \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\) and \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\), they are \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\) and \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib},0}\). No sample-level theorem is asserted for the broad scalar-root map; the estimation, rate, support-recovery, and graph-confidence results remain acyclic-only..
\begin{itemize}
  \item \texttt{p} --- \texttt{integer}; \(\{2,3,\ldots\}\); \texttt{Network dimension.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The number of labeled counting-\allowbreak{}process coordinates.}
  \item \texttt{i} --- \texttt{integer index}; \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Row index.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A receiving-\allowbreak{}coordinate index.}
  \item \texttt{j} --- \texttt{integer index}; \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Column index.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A sending-\allowbreak{}coordinate index.}
  \item \texttt{k} --- \texttt{integer index}; \(\mathbb Z\); \texttt{Time index.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A bin or covariance-\allowbreak{}lag index.}
  \item \texttt{t} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{Time argument.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{Continuous time.}
  \item \texttt{n} --- \texttt{integer}; \(\{3,4,\ldots\}\); \texttt{Sample size.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The number of consecutive observed bins.}
  \item \texttt{Delta} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{Observation resolution.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The known bin width \(\Delta\).
  \item \texttt{alpha} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{Confidence level.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The target familywise error level \(\alpha\).
  \item \texttt{kappa} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,1/2)\); \texttt{Uniformity margin.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed conditioning margin \(\kappa\).
  \item \texttt{kappa0} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,1/2)\); \texttt{Boundary-\allowbreak{}experiment regularity margin.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed nuisance-regularity margin \(\kappa_0\) that is held away from zero while rank, edge strength, and the empty-network boundary may vary.
  \item \texttt{mu} --- \texttt{vector}; \((0,\infty)^p\); \texttt{Structural parameter.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The baseline vector \(\mu\).
  \item \texttt{beta} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{Structural parameter.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The common exponential decay \(\beta\).
  \item \texttt{A} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Structural parameter.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The excitation-amplitude matrix \(A\), where \(A_{ij}\) is the amplitude from label \(j\) to label \(i\).
  \item \texttt{G} --- \texttt{directed graph}; Labeled directed graphs on \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\) in which self-loops are admissible before acyclicity is imposed; \texttt{Causal Hawkes graph.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(G(A)=\{j\to i:A_{ij}>0\}\), including the case \(i=j\); a self-loop is a directed cycle of length one under the graph convention.
  \item \texttt{N} --- \texttt{multivariate simple point process}; Counting measures on \(\mathbb R\times\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Observed continuous-\allowbreak{}time process before binning.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The labeled process \(N=(N_1,\ldots,N_p)\).
  \item \texttt{ell} --- \texttt{predictable vector process}; \((0,\infty)^p\); \texttt{Hawkes intensity.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The conditional-intensity vector \(\ell(t)\) of \(N\).
  \item \texttt{lambda} --- \texttt{vector}; \((0,\infty)^p\); \texttt{First-\allowbreak{}order observable.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The stationary intensity \(\lambda=\mathbb E[\ell(t)]\).
  \item \texttt{D} --- \texttt{diagonal matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Innovation-\allowbreak{}intensity matrix.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(D=\operatorname{diag}(\lambda)\).
  \item \texttt{X} --- \texttt{vector-\allowbreak{}valued stationary sequence}; \(\mathbb N_0^p\); \texttt{Observed bin counts.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(X_k=N(((k+1)\Delta))-N(k\Delta)\).
  \item \texttt{m} --- \texttt{vector}; \((0,\infty)^p\); \texttt{Observable stationary mean count.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(m=\mathbb E[X_0]=\Delta\lambda\).
  \item \texttt{Gamma} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued sequence}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Observable fixed-\allowbreak{}bin covariance lags.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Gamma_k=\operatorname{Cov}(X_k,X_0)\) for \(k\geq 0\).
  \item \texttt{B} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Continuous-\allowbreak{}time generator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(B=A-\beta I_p\).
  \item \texttt{T} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}bin transition.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(T=\exp(B\Delta)\).
  \item \texttt{J} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Bin-\allowbreak{}integration operator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(J=\int_0^\Delta\exp(Bu)\,du=B^{-1}(T-I_p)\).
  \item \texttt{Ccov} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \((0,\infty)\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Continuous-\allowbreak{}time second-\allowbreak{}order object.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The positive-lag covariance density \(C_{+}(t)\) defined by \(\operatorname{Cov}(dN(t),dN(0))=C_{+}(t)\,dt^2\) for \(t>0\).
  \item \texttt{Q} --- \texttt{symmetric matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}definite Lyapunov Gramian.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(Q=\int_0^\infty\exp(Bu)D\exp(B^{\mathsf T}u)\,du\).
  \item \texttt{K} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}lag covariance jump.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(K=A(2\beta I_p-A)Q\).
  \item \texttt{S} --- \texttt{linear subspace}; Subspaces of \(\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Observable active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(S=\operatorname{im}(\Gamma_1)\).
  \item \texttt{r} --- \texttt{integer}; \(\{0,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Observable active rank.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(r=\dim(S)=\operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1)\).
  \item \texttt{O} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times r}\); \texttt{Temporary image coordinate.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} An arbitrary orthonormal basis matrix for \(S\), so \(O^{\mathsf T}O=I_r\) and \(\operatorname{im}(O)=S\).
  \item \texttt{R} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times p}\); \texttt{Full-\allowbreak{}row-\allowbreak{}rank coordinate factor.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(R=O^{\mathsf T}\Gamma_1\).
  \item \texttt{F} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Restricted observable transition.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(F=O^{\mathsf T}\Gamma_2R^{\mathsf T}(RR^{\mathsf T})^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{q} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{Observable decay factor.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(q=\operatorname{tr}(F)/r\).
  \item \texttt{E} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Nilpotent transition increment.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(E=q^{-1}F-I_r\).
  \item \texttt{L} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Finite logarithm of the restricted generator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(L=-\beta I_r+\Delta^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{r-1}(-1)^{h+1}E^h/h\).
  \item \texttt{H} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Inverse restricted bin-\allowbreak{}integration operator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(H=L(F-I_r)^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{Z} --- \texttt{skew-\allowbreak{}symmetric matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Directional covariance jump.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(Z=K-K^{\mathsf T}\).
  \item \texttt{Phi} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Phi_{\Delta,r}:\mathbb R^p\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\to\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Identifying functional.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The explicit rank-\(r\) two-lag recovery map defined in \(\text{def:two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{Psi} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Psi_{\Delta,r}:(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^3\to\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Secondary identifying functional.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The covariance-only recovery map defined in \(\text{def:covariance-only-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{theta} --- \texttt{parameter tuple}; \(\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Full structural target.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\theta=(\beta,A,\mu)\).
  \item \texttt{eta} --- \texttt{centered moment tuple}; \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Population input to the identifying inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\eta=(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\).
  \item \texttt{Y} --- \texttt{stationary vector sequence}; \(\mathbb R^{p+2p^2}\); \texttt{Moment process for dependent-\allowbreak{}data asymptotics.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(Y_k=(X_k,\operatorname{vec}(X_{k+1}X_k^{\mathsf T}),\operatorname{vec}(X_{k+2}X_k^{\mathsf T}))\).
  \item \texttt{xi} --- \texttt{raw moment vector}; \(\mathbb R^{p+2p^2}\); \texttt{Mean of the stationary raw-\allowbreak{}moment process.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\xi=\mathbb E[Y_0]=(m,\operatorname{vec}(M_1),\operatorname{vec}(M_2))\), where \(M_h=\mathbb E[X_hX_0^{\mathsf T}]\) for \(h\in\{1,2\}\).
  \item \texttt{C} --- \texttt{smooth map}; \(\mathcal C:\mathbb R^{p+2p^2}\to\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Centering transformation between the HAC coordinates and the identifying moments.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The raw-to-centered moment map \(\mathcal C(m,\operatorname{vec}(M_1),\operatorname{vec}(M_2))=(m,M_1-mm^{\mathsf T},M_2-mm^{\mathsf T})\), defined in \(\text{def:centering-map}\).
  \item \texttt{zeta} --- \texttt{smooth map}; \(\zeta:\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\to\mathbb R^{p(p-1)/2}\); \texttt{Smooth signed-\allowbreak{}edge functional used before sign inversion.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\zeta(\beta,A,\mu)=(\lambda_jA_{ij}-\lambda_iA_{ji})_{i<j}\), where \(\lambda=(I_p-A/\beta)^{-1}\mu\).
  \item \texttt{Omega} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{(p+2p^2)\times(p+2p^2)}\); \texttt{Long-\allowbreak{}run covariance.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Omega=\sum_{h\in\mathbb Z}\operatorname{Cov}(Y_0,Y_h)\).
  \item \texttt{tn} --- \texttt{positive sequence}; \((0,\infty)^{\mathbb N}\); \texttt{Rank-\allowbreak{}selection tuning.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A rank threshold \(t_n\downarrow0\) with \(\sqrt n\,t_n\to\infty\).
  \item \texttt{edgeThreshold} --- \texttt{positive sequence}; \((0,\infty)^{\mathbb N}\); \texttt{Support-\allowbreak{}selection tuning.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A support threshold \(a_n\downarrow0\) with \(\sqrt n\,a_n\to\infty\).
  \item \texttt{bn} --- \texttt{positive integer sequence}; \(\mathbb N^{\mathbb N}\); \texttt{Heteroskedasticity-\allowbreak{}and-\allowbreak{}autocorrelation tuning.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A long-run covariance bandwidth \(b_n\to\infty\) with \(b_n=o(n^{1/2})\).
  \item \texttt{xiHat} --- \texttt{random raw moment vector}; \(\mathbb R^{p+2p^2}\); \texttt{Empirical raw moments in the HAC coordinates.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat\xi_n=(n-2)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-3}Y_k\).
  \item \texttt{etaHat} --- \texttt{random moment tuple}; \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Empirical identifying moments.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat\eta_n=\mathcal C(\widehat\xi_n)=(\widehat m_n,\widehat\Gamma_{1,n},\widehat\Gamma_{2,n})\).
  \item \texttt{rHat} --- \texttt{random integer}; \(\{0,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Estimated active rank.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat r_n=\sum_{s=1}^p\mathbf 1\{\sigma_s(\widehat\Gamma_{1,n})>t_n\}\).
  \item \texttt{OmegaHat} --- \texttt{random matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{(p+2p^2)\times(p+2p^2)}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}semidefinite long-\allowbreak{}run variance estimator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The Bartlett lag-window heteroskedasticity-and-autocorrelation estimator \(\widehat\Omega_n\) based on \(Y_0,\ldots,Y_{n-3}\) and bandwidth \(b_n\); its finite-sample matrix is positive semidefinite.
  \item \texttt{Mbar} --- \texttt{class of laws}; \texttt{Stationary laws of labeled point processes}; \texttt{Boundary model class.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The acyclic common-decay model including the empty graph, defined in \(\text{def:closure-class}\).
  \item \texttt{Mclass} --- \texttt{class of laws}; \texttt{Stationary laws of labeled point processes}; \texttt{Identification model class.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The nonempty acyclic common-decay model, defined in \(\text{def:model-class}\).
  \item \texttt{Mmargin} --- \texttt{class of laws}; Subclasses of \(Mclass\); \texttt{Uniform-\allowbreak{}inference class.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The fixed-margin class defined in \(\text{def:margin-class}\).
  \item \texttt{Mregular} --- \texttt{class of laws}; Subclasses of \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}\); \texttt{Common comparison class for boundary-\allowbreak{}honest inference.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The nuisance-regular closure class \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0)\) defined in \(\text{def:nuisance-regular-closure}\); it includes \(A=0\) and imposes no rank or edge-separation margin.
  \item \texttt{sRank} --- \texttt{integer rank index}; \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Index for one-\allowbreak{}lag rank strata.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed rank-stratum index \(s\).
  \item \texttt{ThetaBar} --- \texttt{parameter space}; A subset of \(\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Parameter domain including the empty graph.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\) is the structural parameter space inducing \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}\), defined in \(\text{def:parameter-law-topologies}\).
  \item \texttt{ThetaPlus} --- \texttt{parameter space}; A subset of \(\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\); \texttt{Nonempty-\allowbreak{}network parameter domain.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}=\{\theta\in\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}:A\neq0\}\).
  \item \texttt{rhoTheta} --- \texttt{metric}; \texttt{Bounded metric inducing the Euclidean parameter topology.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\rho_{\Theta}(\theta,\theta')=1\wedge\{|\beta-\beta'|+\lVert A-A'\rVert_{\mathrm F}+\lVert\mu-\mu'\rVert_2\}\).
  \item \texttt{Ptheta} --- \texttt{law map}; \(P_{\bullet}:\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\to\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}\); \texttt{Link from structural parameters to observable laws.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(P_{\theta}\) is the stationary labeled Hawkes law induced by \(\theta\).
  \item \texttt{betaLower} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Uniform lower decay bound implied by the decay margin.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\underline\beta=-\Delta^{-1}\log(1-\kappa)\).
  \item \texttt{aBar} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Uniform upper bound on every column sum of excitation amplitudes.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\overline a=\sqrt p\,\kappa^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{nuBar} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Uniform upper bound on the total offspring mean of one event.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\overline\nu=\overline a/\underline\beta\).
  \item \texttt{muBar} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Uniform upper bound on the total immigrant rate.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\overline\mu=\sqrt p\,\kappa^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{Dcl} --- \texttt{nonnegative cluster random variable}; \texttt{Cluster-\allowbreak{}duration variable used to control dependence across bins.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(D_{\mathrm{cl}}\) is the elapsed time from a Hawkes immigrant to the latest event in its Poisson immigration--birth cluster, with value zero for an immigrant having no descendants.
  \item \texttt{cMix} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Derived uniform exponential rate for cluster duration and mixing.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(c_{\mathrm{mix}}=\underline\beta/2\).
  \item \texttt{Cmix} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Derived uniform envelope for cluster-\allowbreak{}crossing and mixing probabilities.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(C_{\mathrm{mix}}=1+\{1+2\overline\mu c_{\mathrm{mix}}^{-1}e^{c_{\mathrm{mix}}\Delta}\}\sum_{d=1}^{p-1}(2\overline a/\underline\beta)^d\).
  \item \texttt{gBranch} --- \texttt{recursively defined function family}; \(g_d:\mathbb R\to(0,\infty)\) for \(d\in\{0,\ldots,p-1\}\); Probability-generating-function bound for a Poisson branching tree with at most \(d\) remaining generations.
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(g_0(z)=e^z\) and \(g_d(z)=\exp[z+\overline\nu\{g_{d-1}(z)-1\}]\) for \(1\leq d\leq p-1\).
  \item \texttt{M8} --- \texttt{positive real scalar}; \texttt{Derived uniform eighth-\allowbreak{}moment envelope.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(M_8=M_8(p,\Delta,\kappa)<\infty\) is the explicit eighth-bin-count constant derived in \(\text{lem:uniform-bin-eighth-moment}\) by differentiating \(g_{p-1}\) through order sixteen and the Poisson-cluster generating functional through order eight.
  \item \texttt{alphaX} --- \texttt{mixing-\allowbreak{}coefficient family}; \(\alpha_X:\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\times\mathbb N_0\to[0,1]\); \texttt{Derived bin-\allowbreak{}count dependence measure.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\alpha_{X,\theta}(h)\) is the strong-mixing coefficient at separation \(h\) of the stationary bin-count sequence \(X\) under \(P_{\theta}\).
  \item \texttt{alphaMix} --- \texttt{mixing-\allowbreak{}coefficient family}; \(\alpha_Y:\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\times\mathbb N_0\to[0,1]\); \texttt{Uniform dependent-\allowbreak{}data regularity measure.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\alpha_{Y,\theta}(h)\) is the strong-mixing coefficient at separation \(h\) of the stationary raw-moment sequence \(Y\) under \(P_{\theta}\).
  \item \texttt{dBL} --- \texttt{probability metric}; \texttt{Bounded-\allowbreak{}Lipschitz metric for the uniform Gaussian approximation.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(d_{\mathrm{BL}}(P,Q)=\sup_{f:\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\leq1,\,\operatorname{Lip}(f)\leq1}|\int f\,dP-\int f\,dQ|\).
  \item \texttt{Wtheta} --- \texttt{covariance-\allowbreak{}matrix family}; \texttt{Varying Gaussian covariance family for signed-\allowbreak{}edge calibration.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(W(\theta)=D(\zeta\circ\Phi_{\Delta,r(\theta)}\circ\mathcal C)_{\xi(\theta)}\Omega(\theta)D(\zeta\circ\Phi_{\Delta,r(\theta)}\circ\mathcal C)_{\xi(\theta)}^{\mathsf T}\), where \(r(\theta)=\operatorname{rank}\{\Gamma_1(\theta)\}\) and the derivative is evaluated through the signed jump before positive parts.
  \item \texttt{rhoLaw} --- \texttt{metric}; \texttt{Topology of total-\allowbreak{}variation convergence for every finite block of binned counts.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\rho_{\mathrm{law}}(P,P')=\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}2^{-h}\{1\wedge\lVert P_{(X_0,\ldots,X_{h-1})}-P'_{(X_0,\ldots,X_{h-1})}\rVert_{\mathrm{TV}}\}\).
  \item \texttt{Oone} --- \texttt{observable map}; \(\mathcal O_1:\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}\to\mathbb R^p\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}lag observation map.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal O_1(\theta)=(m(\theta),\Gamma_1(\theta))\).
  \item \texttt{FiberOne} --- \texttt{set-\allowbreak{}valued map}; \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}lag compatibility fiber.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak F_1(o)=\{\theta'\in\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}:\mathcal O_1(\theta')=o\}\).
  \item \texttt{RankStratum} --- \texttt{parameter subclass}; \texttt{Relatively topologized fixed-\allowbreak{}rank stratum.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Theta_s=\{\theta\in\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}:\operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1(\theta))=s\}\) with the subspace topology inherited from \(\rho_{\Theta}\).
  \item \texttt{Gfamily3} --- \texttt{finite family of directed graphs}; \texttt{Named support families for the unresolved one-\allowbreak{}lag fiber question.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak G_3^{\mathrm{cfd}}\) is the family of all labelings of the three-node directed chain, fork, and diamond support motifs, with absent edges fixed at zero.
  \item \texttt{G0} --- \texttt{directed graph index}; \(\mathfrak G_3^{\mathrm{cfd}}\); \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}lag support-\allowbreak{}stratum index.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed labeled chain, fork, or diamond support \(G_0\).
  \item \texttt{RankSupportStratum} --- \texttt{parameter subclass}; \texttt{Relatively topologized fixed-\allowbreak{}rank,\allowbreak{} fixed-\allowbreak{}support stratum.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Theta_{s,G_0}=\{\theta\in\Theta_{\Delta,3}^{+}:G(A)=G_0,\ \operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1(\theta))=s\}\) with the subspace topology inherited from \(\rho_{\Theta}\).
  \item \texttt{boundaryIndex} --- \texttt{nonnegative functional}; \texttt{Distance scale for rank,\allowbreak{} weak-\allowbreak{}edge,\allowbreak{} and empty-\allowbreak{}graph boundaries.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The boundary-separation index \(\delta(\theta)\) defined in \(\text{def:boundary-risks}\).
  \item \texttt{deltaSep} --- \texttt{nonnegative real scalar}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{Index of the minimax comparison subclass.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A boundary-separation level \(\delta\).
  \item \texttt{deltaSequence} --- \texttt{nonnegative sequence}; \([0,1]^{\mathbb N}\); \texttt{Local-\allowbreak{}to-\allowbreak{}boundary regime.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A sequence \(\delta_n\downarrow0\) of boundary-separation levels.
  \item \texttt{Mdelta} --- \texttt{class of laws}; \texttt{Boundary-\allowbreak{}separated minimax comparison class.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal M_{\kappa_0}(\delta)=\{P_{\theta}\in\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0):\delta(\theta)\geq\delta\}\).
  \item \texttt{Un} --- \texttt{confidence procedure}; \texttt{Joint graph-\allowbreak{}and-\allowbreak{}parameter confidence procedure.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak U_n=(\mathcal G_n,\mathcal T_n)\), where \(\mathcal G_n\) is a random graph set and \(\mathcal T_n\subseteq\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\) is a random parameter set.
  \item \texttt{GraphSpace} --- \texttt{finite graph space}; \texttt{Range of graph confidence sets.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak G_p\) is the set of all labeled directed graphs on \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\) that contain no directed cycle, where a self-loop counts as a cycle of length one.
  \item \texttt{lossInc} --- \texttt{loss function}; \texttt{Graph-\allowbreak{}inclusion loss penalizing false included graphs and noncoverage.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\ell_{\mathrm{inc}}(\mathcal G,G)=|\mathcal G\setminus\{G\}|+|\mathfrak G_p|\mathbf1\{G\notin\mathcal G\}\), where \(\mathfrak G_p\) is the finite set of labeled directed acyclic graphs.
  \item \texttt{diamTheta} --- \texttt{set functional}; \texttt{Parameter-\allowbreak{}set diameter under the bounded Euclidean metric.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\operatorname{diam}_{\Theta}(\mathcal T)=\sup_{\theta,\theta'\in\mathcal T}\rho_{\Theta}(\theta,\theta')\).
  \item \texttt{HonestClass} --- \texttt{class of procedures}; \texttt{Finite-\allowbreak{}sample honest comparison class over the nuisance-\allowbreak{}regular closure.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak H_{n,\kappa_0}(\alpha)=\{\mathfrak U_n:\inf_{\theta:P_{\theta}\in\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0)}P_{\theta}\{G(A)\in\mathcal G_n,\ \theta\in\mathcal T_n\}\geq1-\alpha\}\).
  \item \texttt{AdaptFrontier} --- \texttt{pair of minimax risks}; \texttt{Focal adaptation modulus indexed by boundary separation.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The graph-inclusion and parameter-diameter frontier \(\mathfrak R_{n,\kappa_0}(\delta)=(R_{G,n,\kappa_0}(\delta),R_{\Theta,n,\kappa_0}(\delta))\) defined in \(\text{def:boundary-risks}\).
  \item \texttt{Hone} --- \texttt{research handle}; \texttt{Open information-\allowbreak{}order route.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The one-lag compatibility-fiber handle defined in \(\text{def:one-lag-handle}\).
  \item \texttt{Hboundary} --- \texttt{research handle}; \texttt{Open honest-\allowbreak{}inference route.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The rank-boundary local-experiment handle defined in \(\text{def:boundary-handle}\).
  \item \texttt{ValidEvent} --- \texttt{random event}; \texttt{Events in the sigma-\allowbreak{}field generated by the observed bins}; \texttt{Shared finite-\allowbreak{}sample validity event.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal V_n\) is the measurable valid empirical-inverse event defined in \(\text{def:rank-adaptive-estimator}\); on its complement every downstream inference object uses its declared fallback.
  \item \texttt{thetaHat} --- \texttt{random parameter tuple}; \(\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Total rank-\allowbreak{}adaptive estimator with sentinel fallback.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat\theta_n=\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}(\widehat\eta_n)\) on \(\mathcal V_n\), and \(\widehat\theta_n=(1,0,\mathbf1_p)\) on \(\mathcal V_n^c\); the accompanying report distinguishes an empty estimated rank from abstention.
  \item \texttt{WHat} --- \texttt{random covariance matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p(p-1)/2\times p(p-1)/2}\); \texttt{Total plug-\allowbreak{}in simultaneous signed-\allowbreak{}edge covariance.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathcal V_n\), \(\widehat W_n\) is the Bartlett-HAC sandwich using the derivative of the smooth recovered signed jump; on \(\mathcal V_n^c\), \(\widehat W_n=0\). It is positive semidefinite on every sample.
  \item \texttt{Ghat} --- \texttt{random directed graph}; Labeled directed graphs on \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Estimated Hawkes support.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat G_n=\{j\to i:\widehat A_{ij}>a_n\}\).
  \item \texttt{zHat} --- \texttt{random vector}; \(\mathbb R^{p(p-1)/2}\); \texttt{Total smooth signed-\allowbreak{}edge estimator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\widehat z\) is the recovered upper-triangular signed jump on \(\mathcal V_n\) and zero on \(\mathcal V_n^c\), as defined in \(\text{def:graph-confidence-set}\); under the sentinel convention, \(\widehat z=\zeta(\widehat\theta_n)\) on every sample.
  \item \texttt{Iedge} --- \texttt{random interval family}; Intervals in \(\mathbb R\) indexed by \(1\leq i<j\leq p\); \texttt{Total coordinatewise event underlying conservative graph inclusion.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathcal V_n\), \(I_{ij,n}=[\widehat z_{ij}-c_{n,1-\alpha}/\sqrt n,\widehat z_{ij}+c_{n,1-\alpha}/\sqrt n]\), where \(c_{n,1-\alpha}\) is the generalized-inverse conditional \((1-\alpha)\)-quantile of the unstudentized Gaussian maximum with covariance \(\widehat W_n\), including when that covariance is singular; on \(\mathcal V_n^c\), \(I_{ij,n}=\mathbb R\).
  \item \texttt{Cset} --- \texttt{random set of directed graphs}; Subsets of labeled directed graphs on \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\) that are acyclic under the loop-inclusive convention; \texttt{Total graph uncertainty set with conservative fallback.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal C_n(1-\alpha)\) is the simultaneous sign-inversion set on \(\mathcal V_n\) and the set of all labeled acyclic graphs on \(\mathcal V_n^c\), as defined in \(\text{def:graph-confidence-set}\).
  \item \texttt{DiffEvent} --- \texttt{random event}; \texttt{Events in the sigma-\allowbreak{}field generated by the observed bins}; \texttt{Explicit measurable event on which the full positive-\allowbreak{}part parameter map is differentiable.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal D_n=\mathcal V_n\cap\{Z_{ij}^\star\neq0\text{ for every }1\leq i<j\leq p\}\).
  \item \texttt{VHat} --- \texttt{random covariance matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{(1+p^2+p)\times(1+p^2+p)}\); \texttt{Totalized optional full-\allowbreak{}parameter covariance away from positive-\allowbreak{}part boundaries.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathcal D_n\), \(\widehat V_n=D(\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}\widehat\Omega_nD(\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}^{\mathsf T}\); on \(\mathcal D_n^c\), \(\widehat V_n=0\). It is an optional numerical benchmark and is not used for signed-edge inference.
  \item \texttt{Tmargin} --- \texttt{random parameter set}; Subsets of \(\mathbb R_{+}\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Totalized fixed-\allowbreak{}margin benchmark for parameter diameter,\allowbreak{} separate from graph inference.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal T_n^{\mathrm{fm}}(1-\alpha)\) is the optional full-parameter Wald ellipsoid only on \(\mathcal D_n\) when the ellipsoid is well defined; otherwise it equals \(\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\).
  \item \texttt{Mstable} --- \texttt{class of laws}; \texttt{Stationary laws of labeled point processes}; \texttt{Feedback-\allowbreak{}permitting companion identification class.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The stable common-exponential Hawkes class \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{stab}}\) defined in \(\text{def:stable-common-exponential-class}\).
  \item \texttt{Ifr} --- \texttt{population moment domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Exact domain of the feedback two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\) is the population image of the stable full-rank no-alias class defined in \(\text{def:full-rank-stable-moment-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PhiFull} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Phi^{\mathrm{fr}}_{\Delta,p}:\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Companion identifying functional for feedback networks.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The domain-restricted full-rank stable two-lag recovery map defined in \(\text{def:full-rank-two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{Irestricted} --- \texttt{population moment domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Exact domain of the singular-\allowbreak{}feedback two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\) is the population image of the stable positive-rank zero-diagonal restricted-no-alias class defined in \(\text{def:restricted-rank-stable-moment-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PhiRestricted} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}:\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Companion identifying functional for positive-\allowbreak{}rank singular feedback networks.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The domain-restricted singular-feedback two-lag recovery map defined in \(\text{def:restricted-rank-two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{Lrestricted} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Recovered coordinate matrix of the generator restricted to the active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(L^\sharp=\Delta^{-1}\operatorname{Log}(F)\).
  \item \texttt{ASrestricted} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Recovered coordinate matrix of excitation on the active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(A_S^\sharp=L^\sharp+\beta^\sharp I_r\).
  \item \texttt{Hrestricted} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Inverse restricted bin-\allowbreak{}integration operator for the singular-\allowbreak{}feedback branch.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(H^\sharp=L^\sharp(F-I_r)^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{Prestricted} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Orthogonal projector onto the observable active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(P^\sharp=OO^{\mathsf T}\).
  \item \texttt{Wrestricted} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}definite active-\allowbreak{}image intensity weight.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(W^\sharp=O^{\mathsf T}(D^\sharp)^{-1}O\).
  \item \texttt{Cad} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); Recovered intensity-weighted excitation matrix \(AD\).
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathsf C^\sharp=\{OA_S^\sharp-Z^\sharp(I_p-P^\sharp)(D^\sharp)^{-1}O\}(W^\sharp)^{-1}O^{\mathsf T}+Z^\sharp(I_p-P^\sharp)\).
  \item \texttt{IfrCov} --- \texttt{population covariance domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\)\textasciicircum{}3; \texttt{Exact covariance-\allowbreak{}only domain of the full-\allowbreak{}rank feedback inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\) is the population covariance image of the stable full-rank principal-strip class defined in \(\text{def:full-rank-stable-covariance-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PsiFullCov} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr},0}:\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Covariance-\allowbreak{}only identifying functional for full-\allowbreak{}rank stable feedback networks.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The domain-restricted full-rank covariance-only recovery map defined in \(\text{def:full-rank-covariance-only-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{IrestrictedCov} --- \texttt{population covariance domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\)\textasciicircum{}3; \texttt{Exact covariance-\allowbreak{}only domain of the singular-\allowbreak{}feedback inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\) is the population covariance image of the stable nonempty zero-diagonal restricted-strip class defined in \(\text{def:restricted-rank-stable-covariance-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PsiRestrictedCov} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs},0}:\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Covariance-\allowbreak{}only identifying functional for positive-\allowbreak{}rank zero-\allowbreak{}diagonal stable feedback networks.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The domain-restricted singular-feedback covariance-only recovery map defined in \(\text{def:restricted-rank-covariance-only-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{Iactive} --- \texttt{label subset}; Nonempty proper subsets of \(\{1,\ldots,p\}\); \texttt{Coordinate support of the excitation mechanism.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active observed-label set \(I\) in the isolated invertible-block regime.
  \item \texttt{EI} --- \texttt{ordered coordinate-\allowbreak{}selector matrix}; \(\{0,1\}^{p\times r}\); \texttt{Canonical coordinates for the active observed labels.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For \(I=\{i_1<\cdots<i_r\}\), \(E_I=(e_{i_1},\ldots,e_{i_r})\), so \(E_I^{\mathsf T}E_I=I_r\).
  \item \texttt{AI} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Structural excitation matrix on the active coordinates.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active excitation block \(A_I\) satisfying \(A=E_IA_IE_I^{\mathsf T}\).
  \item \texttt{Lactive} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Continuous-\allowbreak{}time generator on the active coordinate block.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active generator \(L_I=A_I-\beta I_r\).
  \item \texttt{Jactive} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Bin aggregation on the active coordinate block.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active bin-integration operator \(J_I=\int_0^\Delta e^{L_Iu}\,du\).
  \item \texttt{Qactive} --- \texttt{symmetric matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}definite covariance Gramian on the active coordinates.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active Lyapunov Gramian \(Q_I=\int_0^\infty e^{L_Iu}D_Ie^{L_I^{\mathsf T}u}\,du\), where \(D_I=E_I^{\mathsf T}DE_I\).
  \item \texttt{Kactive} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}lag covariance jump in active coordinates.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The active covariance jump \(K_I=A_I(2\beta I_r-A_I)Q_I\).
  \item \texttt{Iisolated} --- \texttt{population moment domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Exact domain of the isolated invertible-\allowbreak{}block two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\) is the population image of stable laws with an invertible excitation block on a nonempty proper coordinate subset, defined in \(\text{def:isolated-invertible-block-stable-moment-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PhiIsolated} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}:\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Identifying functional for a proper active coordinate block with isolated labels.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The isolated invertible-block mean-plus-two-lag recovery map defined in \(\text{def:isolated-invertible-block-two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{IisolatedCov} --- \texttt{population covariance domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\)\textasciicircum{}3; \texttt{Exact covariance-\allowbreak{}only domain of the isolated invertible-\allowbreak{}block inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\) is the population covariance image of the stable isolated invertible-block regime defined in \(\text{def:isolated-invertible-block-stable-covariance-image}\).
  \item \texttt{PsiIsolatedCov} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib},0}:\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{ib}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Covariance-\allowbreak{}only identifying functional for a proper active coordinate block with isolated labels.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The isolated invertible-block covariance-only recovery map defined in \(\text{def:isolated-invertible-block-covariance-only-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{phiKernel} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(\phi:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Structural excitation kernel.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\phi(t)=Ae^{-\beta t}\mathbf 1_{\{t>0\}}\), the causal common-exponential Hawkes kernel.
  \item \texttt{gResponse} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(g:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Input to the Hawkes Wiener-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Hopf equation.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The complete nonsingular conditional-response matrix defined in \(\text{def:complete-hawkes-response}\).
  \item \texttt{Ibroad} --- \texttt{population moment domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^p\times(\mathbb R^{p\times p})^2\); \texttt{Broad exact domain of the Wiener-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Hopf two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\) is the population image of the stable nonzero restricted-strip class defined in \(\text{def:stable-nonzero-restricted-strip-moment-image}\).
  \item \texttt{Jbroad} --- \texttt{population covariance domain}; A subset of \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\)\textasciicircum{}3; \texttt{Broad exact domain of the covariance-\allowbreak{}only Wiener-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Hopf inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\) is the covariance-only population image defined in \(\text{def:stable-nonzero-restricted-strip-covariance-image}\).
  \item \texttt{Lwh} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Recovered coordinate matrix of the generator on the active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(L^{\mathrm{WH}}=\Delta^{-1}\operatorname{Log}(F)\).
  \item \texttt{Hwh} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times r}\); \texttt{Inverse bin-\allowbreak{}integration operator on the active image.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(H^{\mathrm{WH}}=L^{\mathrm{WH}}(F-I_r)^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{Kwh} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Recovered positive-\allowbreak{}lag covariance jump.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(K^{\mathrm{WH}}=O(H^{\mathrm{WH}})^2R\).
  \item \texttt{Cwh} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(C_{+}^{\mathrm{WH}}:(0,\infty)\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Two-\allowbreak{}lag reconstruction of the complete positive-\allowbreak{}lag covariance density.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(C_{+}^{\mathrm{WH}}(t)=Oe^{L^{\mathrm{WH}}t}(H^{\mathrm{WH}})^2R\) for \(t>0\).
  \item \texttt{Dwh} --- \texttt{diagonal matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Recovered innovation-\allowbreak{}intensity matrix.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(D^{\mathrm{WH}}=\operatorname{diag}(m/\Delta)\) for the mean input, and \(D^{\mathrm{WH},0}\) is its zero-lag covariance reconstruction.
  \item \texttt{gWh} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(g^{\mathrm{WH}}:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Observable-\allowbreak{}input response used by the Wiener-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Hopf inverse.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The complete response constructed from \(C_{+}^{\mathrm{WH}}\) and \(D^{\mathrm{WH}}\) in \(\text{def:wiener-hopf-two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{Pwh} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{r\times p}\); \texttt{Active-\allowbreak{}coordinate positive-\allowbreak{}lag covariance-\allowbreak{}jump factor.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(P^{\mathrm{WH}}=(H^{\mathrm{WH}})^2R\).
  \item \texttt{RcalWh} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(\mathcal R^{\mathrm{WH}}:(0,\infty)\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Laplace response transform for the finite root-\allowbreak{}pair evaluator.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal R^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)=\int_0^\infty e^{-xt}C_{+}^{\mathrm{WH}}(t)^{\mathsf T}\,dt=(P^{\mathrm{WH}})^{\mathsf T}(xI_r-(L^{\mathrm{WH}})^{\mathsf T})^{-1}O^{\mathsf T}\) for \(x>0\).
  \item \texttt{OmegaWh} --- \texttt{set of real scalars}; Subsets of \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{Nonsingular domain for the broad scalar-\allowbreak{}root equation.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\Omega^{\mathrm{WH}}=\{x>0:\det[D^{\mathrm{WH}}+\mathcal R^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)]\neq0\}\).
  \item \texttt{AcurveWh} --- \texttt{matrix-\allowbreak{}valued function}; \(\mathcal A^{\mathrm{WH}}:\Omega^{\mathrm{WH}}\to\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Candidate excitation matrix along the positive root domain.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mathcal A^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)=K^{\mathrm{WH}}[D^{\mathrm{WH}}+\mathcal R^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)]^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{betaSharp} --- \texttt{real scalar}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{Recovered common exponential decay.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\beta^{\#}\) is the unique \(x\in\Omega^{\mathrm{WH}}\) satisfying \(\mathcal A^{\mathrm{WH}}(x)O=O(L^{\mathrm{WH}}+xI_r)\).
  \item \texttt{ASharp} --- \texttt{matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{p\times p}\); \texttt{Recovered broad-\allowbreak{}class excitation matrix.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(A^{\#}=\mathcal A^{\mathrm{WH}}(\beta^{\#})\).
  \item \texttt{muSharp} --- \texttt{vector}; \(\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Recovered broad-\allowbreak{}class baseline vector.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \(\mu^{\#}=(I_p-A^{\#}/\beta^{\#})\lambda^{\mathrm{WH}}\).
  \item \texttt{PhiBroad} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}:\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Population identifying functional for stable nonzero restricted-\allowbreak{}strip laws.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The finite-dimensional response-transform and unique-positive-root recovery map defined in \(\text{def:wiener-hopf-two-lag-inverse}\).
  \item \texttt{PsiBroad} --- \texttt{map}; \(\Psi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH},0}:\mathfrak J_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\to\mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p\); \texttt{Covariance-\allowbreak{}only identifying functional for stable nonzero restricted-\allowbreak{}strip laws.}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The covariance-only recovery map that reconstructs the mean and invokes the finite-dimensional map \(\Phi_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{WH}}\), as defined in \(\text{def:wiener-hopf-covariance-only-inverse}\).
\end{itemize}

\section{Assumptions}

\begin{assumption}[ass:dimension]\label{ass:dimension}
\(p\geq2\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Finite labeled Hawkes dimension, \cite{BacryMuzy2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:fixed-bin]\label{ass:fixed-bin}
\(\Delta>0\) is known.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Known regular bin width, \cite{CheyssonLang2022}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:stationarity]\label{ass:stationarity}
The law of \(N\) is invariant under time shifts.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Stationary Hawkes law, \cite{Hawkes1971Spectra}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:hawkes-intensity]\label{ass:hawkes-intensity}
\(\ell_i(t)=\mu_i+\sum_{j=1}^p\int_{(-\infty,t)}A_{ij}e^{-\beta(t-s)}\,N_j(ds)\) almost surely.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Linear exponential multivariate Hawkes intensity, \cite{EtesamiZhangSinghalKiyavash2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:positive-baseline]\label{ass:positive-baseline}
\(\min_{1\leq i\leq p}\mu_i>0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Strictly positive Hawkes baselines, \cite{BacryMuzy2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:positive-decay]\label{ass:positive-decay}
\(\beta>0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Positive exponential decay, \cite{EtesamiZhangSinghalKiyavash2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:nonnegative-excitation]\label{ass:nonnegative-excitation}
\(A_{ij}\geq0\) for every \(i,j\in\{1,\ldots,p\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Excitatory Hawkes kernel, \cite{Hawkes1971Spectra}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:acyclic-support]\label{ass:acyclic-support}
\(G(A)\) contains no directed cycle, with a self-loop counted as a directed cycle of length one.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} This contribution-defining restriction describes labeled feed-forward alarm cascades along documented access, aggregation, and core-network dependencies, where propagation is modeled over a window short enough that slower remediation feedback is external to the Hawkes mechanism. Because self-loops are admissible in \(G(A)\) before the restriction is imposed, acyclicity itself forces \(A_{ii}=0\).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:nonempty-support]\label{ass:nonempty-support}
\(A\neq0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} This identification-specific exclusion is necessary because \(A=0\) yields independent homogeneous Poisson coordinates whose observable law is unchanged for every \(\beta>0\), as characterized separately in \(\text{prop:empty-graph-equivalence}\). It is appropriate for scientific windows selected to contain at least one reproducible cross-label excitation channel.
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:rank-gap]\label{ass:rank-gap}
\(\sigma_r(\Gamma_1)\geq\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Fixed-rank singular-value gap, \cite{KoltchinskiiLounici2016Projectors}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:decay-margin]\label{ass:decay-margin}
\(e^{-\beta\Delta}\in[\kappa,1-\kappa]\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Compact interior parameter restriction, \cite{CheyssonLang2022}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:parameter-envelope]\label{ass:parameter-envelope}
\(\beta+\lVert\mu\rVert_2+\lVert A\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\kappa^{-1}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Compact Hawkes parameter envelope, \cite{CheyssonLang2022}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:intensity-gap]\label{ass:intensity-gap}
\(\min_{1\leq i\leq p}\lambda_i\geq\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Interior stationary-intensity restriction, \cite{BacryMuzy2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:variance-gap]\label{ass:variance-gap}
\(\lambda_{\min}\{\Omega(\theta)\}\geq\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Nonsingular long-run covariance, \cite{ZhangWu2017}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:closure-decay-margin]\label{ass:closure-decay-margin}
\(e^{-\beta\Delta}\in[\kappa_0,1-\kappa_0]\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Compact interior decay restriction on the boundary comparison class, \cite{CheyssonLang2022}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:closure-parameter-envelope]\label{ass:closure-parameter-envelope}
\(\beta+\lVert\mu\rVert_2+\lVert A\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\kappa_0^{-1}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Compact Hawkes envelope on the boundary comparison class, \cite{CheyssonLang2022}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:closure-intensity-gap]\label{ass:closure-intensity-gap}
\(\min_{1\leq i\leq p}\lambda_i\geq\kappa_0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Interior intensity restriction on the boundary comparison class, \cite{BacryMuzy2016}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:closure-variance-gap]\label{ass:closure-variance-gap}
\(\lambda_{\min}\{\Omega(\theta)\}\geq\kappa_0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Nonsingular long-run covariance on the boundary comparison class, \cite{ZhangWu2017}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:hac-bandwidth]\label{ass:hac-bandwidth}
\(b_n\to\infty\) and \(b_n=o(n^{1/2})\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Lag-window bandwidth condition, \cite{ZhangWu2017}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:edge-gap]\label{ass:edge-gap}
\(\min_{i\neq j:Z_{ij}\neq0}|Z_{ij}|\geq\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Beta-min network separation, \cite{WangXieCuozzoMakXie2020}).
\end{assumption}

\section{Definitions}

\begin{definition}[def:closure-class]\label{def:closure-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Acyclic common-\allowbreak{}decay closure class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}=\{P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}:p\geq2,\ \Delta>0,\ N\text{ is stationary with the stated exponential Hawkes intensity},\ \mu_i>0,\ \beta>0,\ A_{ij}\geq0,\ G(A)\text{ contains no directed cycle under the convention that a self-loop is a cycle of length one, for every }i,j\in\{1,\ldots,p\}\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}dimension}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}fixed-\allowbreak{}bin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}stationarity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}hawkes-\allowbreak{}intensity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}baseline}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}decay}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonnegative-\allowbreak{}excitation}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}acyclic-\allowbreak{}support}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:model-class]\label{def:model-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Nonempty acyclic common-\allowbreak{}decay class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG},+}=\{P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}:P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}\text{ satisfies every member property of }\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}\text{ and }A\neq0\text{ for every }p\geq2\text{ and }\Delta>0\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}dimension}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}fixed-\allowbreak{}bin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}stationarity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}hawkes-\allowbreak{}intensity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}baseline}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}decay}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonnegative-\allowbreak{}excitation}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}acyclic-\allowbreak{}support}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonempty-\allowbreak{}support}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:margin-class]\label{def:margin-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Fixed conditioning-\allowbreak{}margin class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}(\kappa)=\{P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}:P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}\text{ satisfies every member property of }\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG},+},\ \sigma_r(\Gamma_1)\geq\kappa,\ e^{-\beta\Delta}\in[\kappa,1-\kappa],\ \beta+\lVert\mu\rVert_2+\lVert A\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\kappa^{-1},\ \min_i\lambda_i\geq\kappa,\ \lambda_{\min}\{\Omega(\theta)\}\geq\kappa,\ \min_{i\neq j:Z_{ij}\neq0}|Z_{ij}|\geq\kappa\text{ for every }i,j\in\{1,\ldots,p\},\ p\geq2,\text{ and }\Delta>0\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}dimension}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}fixed-\allowbreak{}bin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}stationarity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}hawkes-\allowbreak{}intensity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}baseline}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}decay}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonnegative-\allowbreak{}excitation}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}acyclic-\allowbreak{}support}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonempty-\allowbreak{}support}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}rank-\allowbreak{}gap}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}decay-\allowbreak{}margin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}parameter-\allowbreak{}envelope}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}intensity-\allowbreak{}gap}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}variance-\allowbreak{}gap}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}edge-\allowbreak{}gap}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:nuisance-regular-closure]\label{def:nuisance-regular-closure}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Nuisance-\allowbreak{}regular acyclic closure class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0)=\{P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}:P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}\text{ satisfies every member property of }\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}},\ e^{-\beta\Delta}\in[\kappa_0,1-\kappa_0],\ \beta+\lVert\mu\rVert_2+\lVert A\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\kappa_0^{-1},\ \min_{1\leq i\leq p}\lambda_i\geq\kappa_0,\ \lambda_{\min}\{\Omega(\theta)\}\geq\kappa_0\text{ for every }p\geq2\text{ and }\Delta>0\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}dimension}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}fixed-\allowbreak{}bin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}stationarity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}hawkes-\allowbreak{}intensity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}baseline}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}positive-\allowbreak{}decay}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}nonnegative-\allowbreak{}excitation}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}acyclic-\allowbreak{}support}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}closure-\allowbreak{}decay-\allowbreak{}margin}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}closure-\allowbreak{}parameter-\allowbreak{}envelope}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}closure-\allowbreak{}intensity-\allowbreak{}gap}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}closure-\allowbreak{}variance-\allowbreak{}gap}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:population-moments]\label{def:population-moments}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Fixed-\allowbreak{}bin mean and lag covariances}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(X_k=N(((k+1)\Delta))-N(k\Delta),\quad m=\mathbb E[X_0],\quad\Gamma_k=\mathbb E[(X_k-m)(X_0-m)^{\mathsf T}]\) for every \(k\geq0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{N}; \texttt{Delta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:two-lag-inverse]\label{def:two-lag-inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Basis-\allowbreak{}invariant two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(r=\operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1)>0\), choose any orthonormal \(O\) spanning \(S=\operatorname{im}(\Gamma_1)\), set \(R=O^{\mathsf T}\Gamma_1\), \(F=O^{\mathsf T}\Gamma_2R^{\mathsf T}(RR^{\mathsf T})^{-1}\), \(q=\operatorname{tr}(F)/r\), \(\beta^\star=-\Delta^{-1}\log q\), \(E=q^{-1}F-I_r\), \(L^\star=-\beta^\star I_r+\Delta^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{r-1}(-1)^{h+1}E^h/h\), \(H^\star=L^\star(F-I_r)^{-1}\), \(K^\star=O(H^\star)^2R\), \(Z^\star=K^\star-(K^\star)^{\mathsf T}\), \(\lambda^\star=m/\Delta\), \(A_{ii}^\star=0\), \(A_{ij}^\star=\max\{Z_{ij}^\star,0\}/\lambda_j^\star\) for \(i\neq j\), and \(\mu^\star=(I_p-A^\star/\beta^\star)\lambda^\star\); define \(\Phi_{\Delta,r}(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)=(\beta^\star,A^\star,\mu^\star)\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{m}; \texttt{Gamma}; \texttt{Delta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:covariance-only-inverse]\label{def:covariance-only-inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Covariance-\allowbreak{}only inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Apply the transition and logarithm steps of \(\Phi_{\Delta,r}\) to \((\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\), form \(\widetilde C_{+}(t)=Oe^{L^\star t}(H^\star)^2R\), set \(D^\star=\Delta^{-1}[\Gamma_0-\int_0^\Delta(\Delta-t)\{\widetilde C_{+}(t)+\widetilde C_{+}(t)^{\mathsf T}\}\,dt]\), set \(\lambda^\star=\operatorname{diag}(D^\star)\), and complete the edge and baseline steps of \(\Phi_{\Delta,r}\); the output is \(\Psi_{\Delta,r}(\Gamma_0,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Gamma}; \texttt{Delta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:centering-map]\label{def:centering-map}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Raw-\allowbreak{}moment centering map}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(\xi=(m,\operatorname{vec}(M_1),\operatorname{vec}(M_2))\), set \(\mathcal C(\xi)=(m,M_1-mm^{\mathsf T},M_2-mm^{\mathsf T})=(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)=\eta\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{xi}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:rank-adaptive-estimator]\label{def:rank-adaptive-estimator}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Rank-\allowbreak{}adaptive moment inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Set \(\widehat\xi_n=(n-2)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-3}Y_k\), \(\widehat\eta_n=\mathcal C(\widehat\xi_n)=(\widehat m_n,\widehat\Gamma_{1,n},\widehat\Gamma_{2,n})\), and \(\widehat r_n=\sum_{s=1}^p\mathbf1\{\sigma_s(\widehat\Gamma_{1,n})>t_n\}\). Use a fixed Borel-measurable singular-value-decomposition rule to choose the truncated left singular subspace and its orthonormal basis. For \(\widehat r_n>0\), compute every starred intermediate in \(\text{def:two-lag-inverse}\), and let \(\mathcal V_n\) be the event that \(\sigma_{\widehat r_n}(\widehat\Gamma_{1,n})>\sigma_{\widehat r_n+1}(\widehat\Gamma_{1,n})\), with \(\sigma_{p+1}=0\), the resulting \(q\) lies in \((0,1)\), every inverse in that definition exists, every component of \(\lambda^\star=\widehat m_n/\Delta\) used as a divisor is positive, and \(I_p-A^\star/\beta^\star\) is invertible; declare \(\mathcal V_n\) false when \(\widehat r_n=0\). Set \(\widehat\theta_n=\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}(\mathcal C(\widehat\xi_n))\) on \(\mathcal V_n\), and set the measurable sentinel \(\widehat\theta_n=(1,0,\mathbf1_p)\) on \(\mathcal V_n^c\). When \(\widehat r_n=0\), report the empty graph and leave \(\beta\) set-valued over \((0,\infty)\); when \(\widehat r_n>0\) but \(\mathcal V_n\) fails, report abstention. The sentinel is used only to make every numerical random object total.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Y}; \texttt{Delta}; \texttt{tn}; \texttt{n}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:hac-estimator]\label{def:hac-estimator}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Lag-\allowbreak{}window and composite plug-\allowbreak{}in covariance estimators}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For the Bartlett kernel \(w(x)=(1-|x|)_+\), set \(\overline Y_n=(n-2)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-3}Y_k=\widehat\xi_n\) and \(\widehat\Omega_n=\widehat\Xi_0+\sum_{h=1}^{b_n}w(h/b_n)(\widehat\Xi_h+\widehat\Xi_h^{\mathsf T})\), where \(\widehat\Xi_h=(n-2)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-h-3}(Y_k-\overline Y_n)(Y_{k+h}-\overline Y_n)^{\mathsf T}\), with an empty inner sum interpreted as zero. This Bartlett matrix is positive semidefinite for every sample. On \(\mathcal V_n\), define \(\widehat W_n=D(\zeta\circ\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}\widehat\Omega_nD(\zeta\circ\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}^{\mathsf T}\), evaluating the derivative through the smooth representative \(Z^\star=K^\star-(K^\star)^{\mathsf T}\) before positive parts, and set \(\widehat W_n=0\) on \(\mathcal V_n^c\). Define the measurable event \(\mathcal D_n=\mathcal V_n\cap\{Z_{ij}^\star\neq0\text{ for every }1\leq i<j\leq p\}\). On \(\mathcal D_n\), define \(\widehat V_n=D(\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}\widehat\Omega_nD(\Phi_{\Delta,\widehat r_n}\circ\mathcal C)_{\widehat\xi_n}^{\mathsf T}\); on \(\mathcal D_n^c\), set \(\widehat V_n=0\) as a numerical placeholder. The optional \(\widehat V_n\) is not used to define \(\widehat W_n\) or any graph interval.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Y}; \texttt{bn}; \texttt{n}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:graph-confidence-set]\label{def:graph-confidence-set}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Fixed-\allowbreak{}margin graph and parameter confidence sets}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} On \(\mathcal V_n\), let \(\widehat z\) be the vector of upper-triangular entries of the recovered signed jump \(Z^\star=K^\star-(K^\star)^{\mathsf T}\); on \(\mathcal V_n^c\), set \(\widehat z=0\). With the sentinel in \(\text{def:rank-adaptive-estimator}\), this gives the total identity \(\widehat z=\zeta(\widehat\theta_n)\). Let \(\widehat W_n\) be the total positive-semidefinite signed-score covariance in \(\text{def:hac-estimator}\). Conditional on \(\widehat W_n\), for \(\mathcal Z\sim\mathcal N(0,\widehat W_n)\), including singular \(\widehat W_n\), define \(c_{n,1-\alpha}=\inf\{x\in[0,\infty):P(\max_{i<j}|\mathcal Z_{ij}|\leq x\mid\widehat W_n)\geq1-\alpha\}\), with \(\inf\varnothing=+\infty\). On \(\mathcal V_n\), set \(I_{ij,n}=[\widehat z_{ij}-c_{n,1-\alpha}/\sqrt n,\widehat z_{ij}+c_{n,1-\alpha}/\sqrt n]\), and let \(\mathcal C_n(1-\alpha)\) be the set of labeled acyclic graphs which, for every \(i<j\), use no edge only if \(0\in I_{ij,n}\), use \(j\to i\) only if \(I_{ij,n}\cap(0,\infty)\neq\varnothing\), and use \(i\to j\) only if \(I_{ij,n}\cap(-\infty,0)\neq\varnothing\). On \(\mathcal V_n^c\), set every \(I_{ij,n}=\mathbb R\) and let \(\mathcal C_n(1-\alpha)\) be the set of all labeled acyclic graphs. Define \(\mathcal T_n^{\mathrm{fm}}(1-\alpha)\) as the full-parameter Wald ellipsoid only on \(\mathcal D_n\) when \(\widehat V_n\) makes that ellipsoid well defined; on every other sample set \(\mathcal T_n^{\mathrm{fm}}(1-\alpha)=\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\). This optional parameter benchmark is not used in signed-edge or graph coverage.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{thetaHat}; \texttt{OmegaHat}; \texttt{xiHat}; \texttt{alpha}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:parameter-law-topologies]\label{def:parameter-law-topologies}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Parameter and binned-\allowbreak{}law topologies}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Set \(\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}=\{(\beta,A,\mu):P_{\beta,A,\mu}\in\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{DAG}}\}\) and \(\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}=\{\theta\in\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}:A\neq0\}\). Equip \(\overline\Theta_{\Delta,p}\) with \(\rho_{\Theta}(\theta,\theta')=1\wedge\{|\beta-\beta'|+\lVert A-A'\rVert_{\mathrm F}+\lVert\mu-\mu'\rVert_2\}\), and equip the induced binned laws with \(\rho_{\mathrm{law}}(P,P')=\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}2^{-h}\{1\wedge\lVert P_{(X_0,\ldots,X_{h-1})}-P'_{(X_0,\ldots,X_{h-1})}\rVert_{\mathrm{TV}}\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Mbar}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:one-lag-fiber]\label{def:one-lag-fiber}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}lag observable,\allowbreak{} fiber,\allowbreak{} and three-\allowbreak{}node support strata}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(\theta\in\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}\), set \(\mathcal O_1(\theta)=(m(\theta),\Gamma_1(\theta))\) and \(\mathfrak F_1(o)=\{\theta'\in\Theta_{\Delta,p}^{+}:\mathcal O_1(\theta')=o\}\). Let \(\mathfrak G_3^{\mathrm{cfd}}\) contain every labeling of the three-node chain, fork, and diamond motifs. For \(G_0\in\mathfrak G_3^{\mathrm{cfd}}\), set \(\Theta_{s,G_0}=\{\theta\in\Theta_{\Delta,3}^{+}:G(A)=G_0,\ \operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1(\theta))=s\}\). A subset \(U\subseteq\Theta_{s,G_0}\) is relatively open exactly when \(U=V\cap\Theta_{s,G_0}\) for some \(\rho_{\Theta}\)-open \(V\subseteq\Theta_{\Delta,3}^{+}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{ThetaPlus}; \texttt{rhoTheta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:one-lag-handle]\label{def:one-lag-handle}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{One-\allowbreak{}lag compatibility-\allowbreak{}fiber handle}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For each \(G_0\in\mathfrak G_3^{\mathrm{cfd}}\), eliminate \((\beta,A,\mu)\) from the polynomial-exponential equations defining \(\mathfrak F_1\{\mathcal O_1(\theta)\}\); apply real-algebraic stratification within each \(\Theta_{s,G_0}\), enforce Hawkes positivity, and compare distinct surviving parameters in \(\rho_{\mathrm{law}}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{m}; \texttt{Gamma}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:boundary-risks]\label{def:boundary-risks}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Boundary separation and adaptation frontier}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(r(\theta)=\operatorname{rank}(\Gamma_1(\theta))\), set \(\delta(\theta)=0\) when \(A=0\), and otherwise set \(\delta(\theta)=1\wedge\sigma_{r(\theta)}(\Gamma_1(\theta))\wedge\min_{i\neq j:Z_{ij}(\theta)\neq0}|Z_{ij}(\theta)|\). Inside \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0)\), let \(\mathcal M_{\kappa_0}(\delta)=\{P_{\theta}\in\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0):\delta(\theta)\geq\delta\}\). For \(\mathfrak U_n=(\mathcal G_n,\mathcal T_n)\in\mathfrak H_{n,\kappa_0}(\alpha)\), define \(R_{G,n,\kappa_0}(\delta)=\inf_{\mathfrak U_n\in\mathfrak H_{n,\kappa_0}(\alpha)}\sup_{P_{\theta}\in\mathcal M_{\kappa_0}(\delta)}\mathbb E_{\theta}[\ell_{\mathrm{inc}}(\mathcal G_n,G(A))]\) and \(R_{\Theta,n,\kappa_0}(\delta)=\inf_{\mathfrak U_n\in\mathfrak H_{n,\kappa_0}(\alpha)}\sup_{P_{\theta}\in\mathcal M_{\kappa_0}(\delta)}\mathbb E_{\theta}[\operatorname{diam}_{\Theta}(\mathcal T_n)]\); the focal frontier is \(\mathfrak R_{n,\kappa_0}(\delta)=(R_{G,n,\kappa_0}(\delta),R_{\Theta,n,\kappa_0}(\delta))\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Mregular}; \texttt{rhoTheta}; \texttt{alpha}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:boundary-handle]\label{def:boundary-handle}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Rank-\allowbreak{}boundary confidence-\allowbreak{}union handle}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Within \(\overline{\mathcal M}_{\Delta,p}(\kappa_0)\), construct contiguous local experiments for the raw-moment process \(Y\) along sequences indexed by \(\delta_n=\delta(\theta_n)\), while keeping the decay, parameter-envelope, intensity, long-run-variance, derived mixing, derived moment, and bandwidth conditions regular. Derive matching lower and upper bounds for \(\mathfrak R_{n,\kappa_0}(\delta_n)\) under \(\ell_{\mathrm{inc}}\) and \(\operatorname{diam}_{\Theta}\), and invert rank-indexed signed-score tests without first selecting one rank.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Y}; \texttt{Mregular}; \texttt{bn}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:stable-common-exponential-class]\label{def:stable-common-exponential-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Stable common-\allowbreak{}exponential Hawkes class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{stab}}=\{P_{N,\mu,\beta,A}:p\geq2,\ \Delta>0,\ N\text{ is stationary with the stated common-exponential Hawkes intensity},\ \mu_i>0,\ \beta>0,\ A_{ij}\geq0\text{ for every }i,j\in\{1,\ldots,p\},\ \rho(A/\beta)<1\}\), where \(\rho\) denotes spectral radius. No acyclicity, zero-diagonal, orientation, or full-rank condition is part of this class definition.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{N}; \texttt{Delta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:full-rank-stable-moment-image]\label{def:full-rank-stable-moment-image}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Full-\allowbreak{}rank stable no-\allowbreak{}alias population moment image}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Let \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\) be the population moment image
\[
 \left\{(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2):\begin{array}{l}
 \text{there exists }(\beta,A,\mu)\text{ inducing a law in }\mathcal M_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{stab}},\\
 \det(A)\neq0,\quad
 \operatorname{spec}\{\Delta(A-\beta I_p)\}\subset\{z\in\mathbb C:|\operatorname{Im}z|<\pi\},\\
 \text{and }m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2\text{ are that law's population moments}
 \end{array}\right\}.
\]
This is an image-defined domain, not an additional observable assumption.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Mstable}; \texttt{m}; \texttt{Gamma}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:full-rank-two-lag-inverse]\label{def:full-rank-two-lag-inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Domain-\allowbreak{}restricted full-\allowbreak{}rank stable two-\allowbreak{}lag inverse}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(\eta=(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2)\in\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\), set
\[
 \lambda^\dagger=m/\Delta,\quad D^\dagger=\operatorname{diag}(\lambda^\dagger),\quad
 T^\dagger=\Gamma_2\Gamma_1^{-1},\quad
 B^\dagger=\Delta^{-1}\operatorname{Log}(T^\dagger),
\]
\[
 J^\dagger=(B^\dagger)^{-1}(T^\dagger-I_p),\quad
 K^\dagger=(J^\dagger)^{-2}\Gamma_1,\quad
 Q^\dagger=\int_0^\infty e^{B^\dagger u}D^\dagger e^{(B^\dagger)^{\mathsf T}u}\,du,
\]
and
\[
 s^\dagger=p^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\!\left\{K^\dagger(Q^\dagger)^{-1}+(B^\dagger)^2\right\},\quad
 \beta^\dagger=(s^\dagger)^{1/2},\quad
 A^\dagger=B^\dagger+\beta^\dagger I_p,\quad
 \mu^\dagger=(I_p-A^\dagger/\beta^\dagger)\lambda^\dagger,
\]
where the square root is the positive real square root. Define the domain-restricted map
\[
 \Phi^{\mathrm{fr}}_{\Delta,p}:\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{\mathrm{fr}}\longrightarrow
 \mathbb R_+\times\mathbb R^{p\times p}\times\mathbb R^p,
 \qquad
 \Phi^{\mathrm{fr}}_{\Delta,p}(\eta)=(\beta^\dagger,A^\dagger,\mu^\dagger).
\]
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{Ifr}; \texttt{m}; \texttt{Gamma}; \texttt{Delta}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:restricted-rank-stable-moment-image]\label{def:restricted-rank-stable-moment-image}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Positive-\allowbreak{}rank zero-\allowbreak{}diagonal restricted-\allowbreak{}no-\allowbreak{}alias population moment image}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} Let \(\mathfrak I_{\Delta,p}^{0,+,\mathrm{rs}}\) be the population moment image
\[
 \left\{(m,\Gamma_1,\Gamma_2):\begin{array}{l}
